## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

**Birth of children**: Boys are symbolized by the Sun or Mars. The V house cusp appears very frequently in births. The AS is the most frequent angle. The actual planetary combination for a birth is Mars-Jupiter. An unexpected birth (premature) is with Uranus.

# Birth of Daughter

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H4, H1, H5, VEN, MON, JUP, NNO  
**SECONDARY**: MAR, URA, PLU, SUN, MER

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: Can expect births of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects).
- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: Frequent to find births of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects). The opposition can also indicate births.
- **AS-Mercury / Mercury-AS**: Frequent to find births of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects).
- **AS-Venus / Venus-AS**: Find the births of children (gender_unspecified) (in all possible aspects).
- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: The only positive events that appear with this combination are the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **AS-Jupiter / Jupiter-AS**: It is very common in the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: We find the birth of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects). Occasionally, the opposition can generate births of children.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: In some harmonic aspects and occasionally in the opposition, it indicates the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: We often find the birth of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and even in some oppositions).
- **AS-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-AS**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: births (gender_unspecified).
- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: We find birth of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions).
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: We find births of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions).
- **MC-Mercury / Mercury-MC**: We find births of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions).
- **MC-Venus / Venus-MC**: We always find births of children (gender_unspecified).
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: The only positive events that appear with this combination are births of children (gender_unspecified).
- **MC-Jupiter / Jupiter-MC**: It is a very frequent direction in the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: We find the birth of children (gender_unspecified) (in conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions).
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: The birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **MC-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-MC**: Positive events are more frequent: births (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Moon / Moon-Sun**: On a personal level, we find the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Mercury / Mercury-Sun**: On a personal level, we come across the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Venus / Venus-Sun**: On a personal level, we observe the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: The only positive events observed with this combination, especially in conjunctions and harmonic aspects, are the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Jupiter / Jupiter-Sun**: On a personal level, we observe the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Uranus / Uranus-Sun**: On a personal level, we come across births of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Pluto / Pluto-Sun**: Occasionally, the only positive event that appears with this combination, especially in conjunctions and harmonic aspects, is the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Sun**: Among the positive events, we find the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Sun-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Sun**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: births (gender_unspecified).
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: On a personal level, we often find the birth of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Moon-Venus / Venus-Moon**: The birth of children (gender_unspecified) is frequent.
- **Moon-Jupiter / Jupiter-Moon**: On a personal level, we come across births of children (gender_unspecified).

- **Mercury-Venus / Venus-Mercury**: The birth of children (gender_unspecified) is frequent.

- **Mercury-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mercury**: We also observe the birth of children (gender_unspecified).

- **Jupiter-Uranus / Uranus-Jupiter**: To a lesser extent, this direction signals births of children (gender_unspecified).
- **Jupiter-Pluto / Pluto-Jupiter**: On a personal level, we find births of children (gender_unspecified).

- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: Perhaps, it sometimes signals a birth (general_birth_reference).

- **Uranus-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Uranus**: On a personal level, births of children (gender_unspecified) are common.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: On a personal level, more events appear, both positive and negative; births and deaths are frequent (general_birth_reference).
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: On a personal level, we may find a birth in the family (general_birth_reference).

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

**Birth of children**: Girls are symbolized by Venus (secondarily by the Moon). The V house cusp appears very frequently in births. The AS is the most frequent angle. The actual planetary combination for a birth is Mars-Jupiter. An unexpected birth (premature) is with Uranus.

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: Positive events, such as weddings (sex_unspecified) (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects).
- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: We find weddings (sex_unspecified) (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects). The opposition can also indicate weddings.
- **AS-Venus / Venus-AS**: We find positive events, especially: weddings (sex_unspecified) (in all of the possible aspects).
- **AS-Jupiter / Jupiter-AS**: Appears in weddings of the individual (sex_unspecified).
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: Occasionally, the opposition can generate weddings (sex_unspecified).
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: Less frequently, marriage engagements (sex_unspecified) can occur.

- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: We find some weddings or marriage engagements (sex_unspecified) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions).
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: We find weddings (of the individual) (sex_unspecified) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions).

- **MC-Jupiter / Jupiter-MC**: To a lesser extent, it appears in weddings of the individual (sex_unspecified).

- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: Occasionally, in the conjunction and harmonic aspects, we find this direction in some weddings (sex_unspecified).
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: We often find positive events: positive personal happenings, such as weddings (of the individual) (sex_unspecified) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and even in some oppositions).
- **MC-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-MC**: Positive events are more frequent: weddings (sex_unspecified) (in conjunctions and in some oppositions).
- **Sun-Moon / Moon-Sun**: On a personal level, we find weddings (of the individual) (sex_unspecified).

- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: To a lesser extent the wedding of the individual (sex_unspecified).

- **Moon-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Moon**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: weddings (sex_unspecified).
- **Mercury-Venus / Venus-Mercury**: On a personal level, weddings, marriage engagements are frequent (sex_unspecified).
- **Mercury-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mercury**: We also observe weddings and marriage engagements (sex_unspecified).

- **Venus-Jupiter / Jupiter-Venus**: On a personal level, this direction seems to be more active. We find weddings (of the individual or of their relatives), marriage engagements (sex_unspecified).

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

**Marriage**: Male: the DS with Venus or the Moon. MC directions with the Sun or Jupiter are frequent in those cases where the partner achieves a rise in his social standing.

- **Moon-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Moon**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: weddings (sex_unspecified).
- **Mercury-Venus / Venus-Mercury**: On a personal level, weddings, marriage engagements are frequent (sex_unspecified).
- **Mercury-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mercury**: We also observe weddings and marriage engagements (sex_unspecified).
- **Mercury-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mercury**: To a lesser extent some marriage engagements (sex_unspecified).

- **Venus-Mars / Mars-Venus**: On a personal level, the most common types of events are weddings and marriage engagements (of the individual or of their relatives) (sex_unspecified).
- **Venus-Jupiter / Jupiter-Venus**: On a personal level, this direction seems to be more active. We find weddings (of the individual or of their relatives), marriage engagements (sex_unspecified).
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: To a lesser extent, weddings (of the individual) (sex_unspecified) are observed.

- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: On a personal level, we come across some weddings or marriage engagements (sex_unspecified).
- **Mars-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mars**: The only exception is the wedding of the individual or of a family member (sex_unspecified).
- **Jupiter-Uranus / Uranus-Jupiter**: On a personal level, we come across many trips and some weddings, either of the individual or of their relatives (sex_unspecified).

- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: On a personal level, we find the wedding of the individual or of their relatives (sex_unspecified).
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: On a personal level, we observe positive events such as weddings (sex_unspecified).

**Marriage**: Female: the DS with the Sun or Jupiter. MC directions with the Sun or Jupiter are frequent in those cases where the partner achieves a rise in his social standing.

- **AS-Jupiter / Jupiter-AS**: To a lesser extent, it appears in weddings of their children.
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: We often find positive events such as weddings (of their children) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and even in some oppositions).
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: We find weddings (of their children) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions).
- **MC-Mercury / Mercury-MC**: We find weddings (of their children) (in conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions).
- **MC-Jupiter / Jupiter-MC**: To a lesser extent, it appears in weddings of their children.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: We find some weddings (of their children) (in conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions).
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: We often find positive events: positive personal happenings, such as weddings (of their children) (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and even in some oppositions).
- **Sun-Moon / Moon-Sun**: On a personal level, we find weddings (of their children).
- **Sun-Mercury / Mercury-Sun**: On a personal level, we come across weddings (of their children).
- **Sun-Venus / Venus-Sun**: On a personal level, we observe weddings (of their children).
- **Sun-Jupiter / Jupiter-Sun**: On a personal level, we observe weddings (of their children).
- **Sun-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Sun**: Among the positive events, we find weddings (of their children).
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: To a lesser extent the wedding of their children.
- **Moon-Jupiter / Jupiter-Moon**: Occasionally the wedding of their children.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: To a lesser extent the wedding of their children.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: Among the positive events, we find weddings (of their children).
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: To a lesser extent, weddings (of their children) are observed.
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: On a personal level, we find weddings (of their children).

# Positive Travel

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H9, MON, MER, URA, JUP  
**SECONDARY**: VEN, SUN, NNO

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: Some trips can occur.
- **AS-Mercury / Mercury-AS**: Trips are also common.
- **AS-Venus / Venus-AS**: We find some trips.
- **AS-Jupiter / Jupiter-AS**: It is very common in trips.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: We find even some trips (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects).
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: A lot of trips can materialize.
- **AS-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-AS**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: trips.
- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: Changes of address and trips are also observed.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: We find trips (in conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions).
- **MC-Mercury / Mercury-MC**: With these directions, trips are frequent.
- **MC-Venus / Venus-MC**: We always find some trips.
- **MC-Jupiter / Jupiter-MC**: It is a very frequent direction in trips.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: We find trips (in conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions).
- **MC-Neptune / Neptune-MC**: In harmonic aspects and in some conjunctions, trips, especially long distance, are frequent.
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: It can also appear in some trips.
- **Sun-Mercury / Mercury-Sun**: With this direction, trips are frequent.
- **Sun-Venus / Venus-Sun**: Trips are also common.
- **Sun-Jupiter / Jupiter-Sun**: Trips are also common.
- **Sun-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Sun**: An occasional trip is also observed.
- **Sun-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Sun**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: trips.
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: With this direction, trips are common.
- **Moon-Venus / Venus-Moon**: With this direction, we come across some trips.
- **Moon-Jupiter / Jupiter-Moon**: With this direction, trips are frequent.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: With this direction, trips are frequent.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: There are also occasional trips.
- **Mercury-Venus / Venus-Mercury**: With this direction, we observe some trips.
- **Mercury-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mercury**: With this direction, trips are frequent.
- **Mercury-Uranus / Uranus-Mercury**: On a personal level, trips are frequent.
- **Mercury-Neptune / Neptune-Mercury**: The only positive events that sometimes appear with this combination, especially in harmonic aspects, are some trips.
- **Mercury-Pluto / Pluto-Mercury**: On the personal level, we observe some trips.
- **Mercury-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mercury**: Among the positive events, we find many trips.
- **Mercury-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mercury**: This direction can also involve some trips.
- **Venus-Jupiter / Jupiter-Venus**: This direction involves many trips.
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: Some trips are also observed.
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: Also some trips.
- **Venus-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Venus**: This direction may involve some trips.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: On a personal level, we come across many trips. As an example: Mars 0° Jupiter: trip to the USSR (Che Guevara).
- **Jupiter-Uranus / Uranus-Jupiter**: On a personal level, we come across many trips.
- **Jupiter-Pluto / Pluto-Jupiter**: Trips are also common.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: The only positive events that appear are some occasional trips.
- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: Among other minor positive events, we also come across some trips.
- **Jupiter-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Jupiter**: We also find some trips and other minor positive events.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: On a personal level, we observe positive events such as trips.
- **Uranus-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Uranus**: We also find some trips and other minor positive events.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: We also observe some trips.
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: Trips are also found.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Voyage (trips, journeys): III or IX house cusp with the Moon, Mercury or Uranus. Neptune overseas (also flights). Jupiter stands for long journeys (or stays) in foreign countries. Uranus symbolizes new impressions, incidents and accidents.

- **Neptune-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Neptune**: The only positive event I have seen with this direction is an overseas trip.

Voyage (trips, journeys): III or IX house cusp with the Moon, Mercury or Uranus. Neptune overseas (also flights). Jupiter stands for long journeys (or stays) in foreign countries. Uranus symbolizes new impressions, incidents and accidents.

# Success or Elected

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H3, SUN, JUP, MON, MER, URA, VEN  
**SECONDARY**: NNO

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: Some politicians are elected and some kings accede to the throne with these directions. In some cases, we can expect other positive events, such as satisfactory sentimental relationships.
- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: All types of positive family events. At times, the square generates positive events. Occasional successes.
- **AS-Mercury / Mercury-AS**: Some professional successes or achievements can occur. (The opposition is not always negative, as it sometimes operates like a conjunction and harmonic aspects.)
- **AS-Venus / Venus-AS**: We find positive events, especially: sentimental relationships.
- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: Sporting achievements
- **AS-Jupiter / Jupiter-AS**: We only find positive events, especially: professional successes, awards and all kinds of achievements.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: In conjunctions and harmonic aspects, we find some professional successes, awards. Occasionally, the opposition can generate some professional successes.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: Less frequently, other positive events such as jobs, occasional successes can occur.
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: Some professional successes can happen.
- **AS-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-AS**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: worldly successes.
- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: In conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions, we find successes and professional promotions.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: In conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions, we find professional successes and promotions.
- **MC-Mercury / Mercury-MC**: In conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions, we find professional successes and promotions.
- **MC-Venus / Venus-MC**: We always find positive events, especially: successes, prizes and professional recognitions.
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: A hard-fought victory for a politician.Sporting achievements.
- **MC-Jupiter / Jupiter-MC**: In this direction, we find professional successes, prizes and achievements of all types.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: In conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions, we find professional successes and promotions.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: Occasionally, in the conjunction and harmonic aspects, we find this direction in a professional advancement or promotion.
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: In conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and even in some oppositions, we often find positive events: professional successes.
- **MC-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-MC**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: professional successes, promotions or prizes.
- **Sun-Moon / Moon-Sun**: On a personal level, we find sentimental relationships.
- **Sun-Mercury / Mercury-Sun**: On a professional level, we find promotions or successes.
- **Sun-Venus / Venus-Sun**: On a professional level, we find successes, prizes or promotions.
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: Sporting achievements.
- **Sun-Jupiter / Jupiter-Sun**: On a professional level, we find successes, prizes, promotions or achievements of all kinds.
- **Sun-Uranus / Uranus-Sun**: On a professional level, we find successes, prizes or promotions.
- **Sun-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Sun**: Among the positive events, we find romantic relationships.
- **Sun-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Sun**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: professional successes or career promotions.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: Among the positive events, we find romantic relationships.
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: On a professional level, promotions or successes can occur.
- **Moon-Venus / Venus-Moon**: On a professional level, we find successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds.
- **Moon-Jupiter / Jupiter-Moon**: All kinds of favorable family events.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: On a professional level, successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds are possible.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: Among the positive events, we find professional successes.
- **Moon-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Moon**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: professional successes or promotions.
- **Mercury-Venus / Venus-Mercury**: On a personal level, romantic relationships are frequent. In addition, successes in studies and positive events in relation to children are possible.
- **Mercury-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mercury**: It is a relatively common direction when a politician wins an election.
- **Mercury-Uranus / Uranus-Mercury**: This combination is quite common in the case of politicians who are elected to office.
- **Mercury-Pluto / Pluto-Mercury**: On a professional level, some promotions and occasional successes are found.
- **Mercury-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mercury**: Among the positive events, we find professional successes.
- **Mercury-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mercury**: Successes in studies or politics. This combination appears among positive events in general, both personal and professional.
- **Venus-Mars / Mars-Venus**: On a professional level, successes are more frequent than failures.
- **Venus-Jupiter / Jupiter-Venus**: On a professional level, some successes and recognitions are possible.
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: On a professional level, we come across successes, promotions, unexpected achievements and even prizes.
- **Venus-Neptune / Neptune-Venus**: Positive events we can expect are those related to art or similar subjects, but it is very rare that such a direction generates a favorable event.
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: On a professional level, we mostly find positive events, such as specific successes or promotions.
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: On a personal level, we find sentimental relationships.
- **Venus-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Venus**: On a professional level, we find successes and achievements of all kinds.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: As an example: Jupiter 0° Mars: appointment as Prince of Wales (Prince Charles).
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: Some occasional sporting achievements.
- **Jupiter-Saturn / Saturn-Jupiter**: On a professional level we find some positive events, such as promotions or responsibilities, and some other achievements.
- **Jupiter-Uranus / Uranus-Jupiter**: On a professional level, this is a very positive direction and we always find successes, promotions and unexpected achievements. It commonly indicates prizes and awards of all kinds.
- **Jupiter-Pluto / Pluto-Jupiter**: On a professional level, we observe occasional successes or promotions, some distinctions and other positive events.
- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: Among the positive events, this direction indicates professional successes of all types, promotions and awards.
- **Jupiter-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Jupiter**: On a professional level, successes and recognitions of all kinds are likely.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: Perhaps, it sometimes signals a personal advantage or a small professional achievement.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: On a professional level, we find both successes and defeats: from all kinds of achievements and benefits.
- **Uranus-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Uranus**: This is a clearly beneficial direction. On a professional level, successes, promotions and benefits of any type are possible.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: On a professional level, we find all kinds of successes.
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: On a professional level, both successes and failures can take place.

**Short Relationships**

- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: "open relationships" - reason: Term is too vague to map to marriage/divorce categories.; candidate_categories: none.
- **Venus-Mars / Mars-Venus**: "fleeting relationships"
- **Venus-Jupiter / Jupiter-Venus**: "fleeting relationships"
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: "fleeting relationships"
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: "fleeting relationships"

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Decoration: MC (or AS) with Jupiter, the Sun or the Moon. Honors after death: VIII house cusp mainly with Jupiter.

Elections: III house cusp, MC or AS with the Sun, the Moon, Mercury, Jupiter or Uranus.

Love affairs: Male: V or AS, mainly with Venus (sometimes assisted by Mars or Pluto). Female: V or AS, mainly with Mars or Pluto (sometimes assisted by Venus). In secrecy (or betraying the current partner): Venus-Neptune (male), Mars-Neptune (female).

Political success: Directions with MC, III house cusp, Jupiter, the Sun, the Moon, Mercury or Uranus.

Raise in salary: II house cusp with Venus or Jupiter. Interplanetary directions: Venus-Jupiter, Mercury-Venus and Mercury-Jupiter.

Succession (in business, to the throne or other): VIII house cusp with Jupiter. Jupiter-Pluto combinations.

Wins: V house cusp or AS with Jupiter, Uranus or Venus. Same planets in interplanetary directions.

# Move Home

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H4, H1, H3, MER, MON, NNO, JUP, SUN, VEN  
**SECONDARY**: URA

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: Changes of address are also observed.
- **MC-Mercury / Mercury-MC**: Occasional changes of address can occur.
- **MC-Jupiter / Jupiter-MC**: Occasionally, it indicates changes of address.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: We find changes of address (in conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions).
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: It can also appear in some changes of address.
- **Sun-Mercury / Mercury-Sun**: Occasional changes of address can occur.
- **Sun-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Sun**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: moves.
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: Changes of address are found.
- **Moon-Venus / Venus-Moon**: Occasionally changes of address.
- **Moon-Jupiter / Jupiter-Moon**: Changes of address can take place.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: Changes of address can take place.
- **Mercury-Venus / Venus-Mercury**: Occasional changes of address.
- **Mercury-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mercury**: Changes of address are possible.
- **Mercury-Neptune / Neptune-Mercury**: Changes of address, though they may be unfavorable.
- **Mercury-Pluto / Pluto-Mercury**: Changes of address.
- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: Changes of address.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: Changes of address.
- **Neptune-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Neptune**: Unfavorable changes of address.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

None

# Job Promotion

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H3, H2, SUN, JUP, MON, MER, URA, VEN  
**SECONDARY**: H11, PLU, NNO

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: We find all types of successes and professional promotions (in conjunctions and harmonic aspects).
- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: Some trips and professional achievements can occur.
- **AS-Mercury / Mercury-AS**: Some professional successes or achievements can occur.
- **AS-Venus / Venus-AS**: Worldly successes of all types.
- **AS-Jupiter / Jupiter-AS**: We only find positive events, especially: professional successes, awards and all kinds of achievements.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: In conjunctions and harmonic aspects, we find some professional successes, awards. Occasionally, the opposition can generate some professional successes.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: Less frequently, other positive events such as jobs, occasional successes can occur.
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: Some professional successes can happen.
- **AS-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-AS**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: worldly successes.
- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: In conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions, we find successes and professional promotions.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: In conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and in oppositions, we find professional successes and promotions.
- **MC-Mercury / Mercury-MC**: In conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions, we find professional successes and promotions.
- **MC-Venus / Venus-MC**: We always find positive events, especially: successes, prizes and professional recognitions.
- **MC-Jupiter / Jupiter-MC**: In this direction, we find professional successes, prizes and achievements of all types.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: In conjunctions, harmonic aspects and oppositions, we find professional successes and promotions.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: Occasionally, in the conjunction and harmonic aspects, we find this direction in a professional advancement or promotion.
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: In conjunctions, in harmonic aspects and even in some oppositions, we often find positive events: professional successes.
- **MC-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-MC**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: professional successes, promotions or prizes.
- **Sun-Moon / Moon-Sun**: On a professional level, we find successes, prizes or promotions.
- **Sun-Mercury / Mercury-Sun**: On a professional level, we find promotions or successes.
- **Sun-Venus / Venus-Sun**: On a professional level, we find successes, prizes or promotions.
- **Sun-Jupiter / Jupiter-Sun**: On a professional level, we find successes, prizes, promotions or achievements of all kinds.
- **Sun-Uranus / Uranus-Sun**: On a professional level, we find successes, prizes or promotions.
- **Sun-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Sun**: Among the positive events, we find professional successes.
- **Sun-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Sun**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: professional successes or career promotions.
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: On a professional level, promotions or successes can occur.
- **Moon-Venus / Venus-Moon**: On a professional level, we find successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds.
- **Moon-Jupiter / Jupiter-Moon**: On a professional level, this direction is very positive, as we find successes, awards, promotions and achievements of all kinds.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: On a professional level, successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds are possible.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: Among the positive events, we find professional successes.
- **Moon-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Moon**: In conjunctions and in some oppositions, positive events are more frequent: professional successes or promotions.
- **Mercury-Venus / Venus-Mercury**: On a professional level, we find successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds.
- **Mercury-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mercury**: On a professional level, we find successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds.
- **Mercury-Uranus / Uranus-Mercury**: On a professional level, successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds are possible.
- **Mercury-Pluto / Pluto-Mercury**: On a professional level, some promotions and occasional successes are found.
- **Mercury-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mercury**: Among the positive events, we find professional successes.
- **Mercury-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mercury**: On a professional level, successes, promotions and achievements of all kinds are common.
- **Venus-Mars / Mars-Venus**: On a professional level, successes are more frequent than failures.
- **Venus-Jupiter / Jupiter-Venus**: On a professional level, some successes and recognitions are possible.
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: On a professional level, we come across successes, promotions, unexpected achievements and even prizes.
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: On a professional level, we mostly find positive events, such as specific successes or promotions.
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: Among the positive events, we observe professional successes, promotions and various recognitions.
- **Venus-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Venus**: On a professional level, we find successes and achievements of all kinds.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: On a professional level, successes are much more frequent than failures: from promotions to even awards, especially with conjunctions and harmonic aspects.
- **Jupiter-Saturn / Saturn-Jupiter**: On a professional level we find some positive events, such as promotions or responsibilities, and some other achievements.
- **Jupiter-Uranus / Uranus-Jupiter**: On a professional level, this is a very positive direction and we always find successes, promotions and unexpected achievements. It commonly indicates prizes and awards of all kinds.
- **Jupiter-Pluto / Pluto-Jupiter**: On a professional level, we observe occasional successes or promotions, some distinctions and other positive events.
- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: Among the positive events, this direction indicates professional successes of all types, promotions and awards.
- **Jupiter-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Jupiter**: On a professional level, successes and recognitions of all kinds are likely.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: Perhaps, it sometimes signals a personal advantage or a small professional achievement.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: On a professional level, we find both successes and defeats: from all kinds of achievements and benefits.
- **Uranus-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Uranus**: This is a clearly beneficial direction. On a professional level, successes, promotions and benefits of any type are possible.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: On a professional level, we find all kinds of successes.
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: On a professional level, both successes and failures can take place.

## Other

Promotion: MC with usually the Sun or Jupiter. Interplanetary directions of the Sun, Jupiter, and sometimes the Moon.

Raise in salary: II house cusp with Venus or Jupiter. Interplanetary directions: Venus-Jupiter, Mercury-Venus and Mercury-Jupiter.

Succession (in business, to the throne or other): VIII house cusp with Jupiter. Jupiter-Pluto combinations.

# Gambling Gain

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H2, H5, JUP, VEN, URA  
**SECONDARY**: PLU

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **Sun-Jupiter / Jupiter-Sun**: Occasionally, prizes in gambling, bets or lotteries are found.
- **Venus-Jupiter / Jupiter-Venus**: Occasionally, extraordinary monetary gains, through gambling or other ways.
- **Jupiter-Uranus / Uranus-Jupiter**: It commonly indicates prizes and awards of all kinds, and even gambling winnings.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Wins: V house cusp or AS with Jupiter, Uranus or Venus. Same planets in interplanetary directions.

# Army Promotion

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H3, SUN, MAR, PLU, JUP, MON, MER, URA  
**SECONDARY**: H2, H11, VEN, NNO

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: The only positive events that appear with this combination are military promotions.
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: The only positive events that appear with this combination are military promotions.
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: Occasionally, it also appears in case of joining or getting a promotion in the army.
- **Moon-Mars / Mars-Moon**: Exceptionally, we observe positive events such as a military promotion.
- **Mercury-Mars / Mars-Mercury**: The only positive events that sometimes appear with this combination... are an enlistment or promotion in the army.
- **Mars-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mars**: Except for military promotions, which often occur with this direction.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

None

# Death of Father or Grandfather

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H8, SAT, SUN, NEP, PLU, MAR, NNO  
**SECONDARY**: H12, URA, MON

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find deaths of relatives or friends.
- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find deaths of people surrounding the individual.
- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: We find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Saturn / Saturn-AS**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: In inharmonic aspects and in some conjunctions, we come across some deaths of relatives and friends.
- **AS-Neptune / Neptune-AS**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: In this direction, we find personal and professional setbacks of all types, illnesses, accidents or deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: We also find negative events, such as the ones that occur with the square and some oppositions: illnesses, deaths, divorces and setbacks of all kinds.
- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: Less frequently, in conjunctions and oppositions, we come across deaths of relatives, especially of males.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: In the squares, in some oppositions and occasionally in some conjunctions, we observe negative events of different types: deaths of relatives or friends.
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: In this direction, we find deaths, both of the individual and of their family and friends.
- **MC-Saturn / Saturn-MC**: In this direction, we find personal events such as deaths, both of the individual and of their family and friends.
- **MC-Neptune / Neptune-MC**: On a personal level, it is also common to find problems of all kinds: deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: On a personal level, it often also comes across in problems of all types: deaths either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: We also come across negative events, such as the ones that occur in squares and in some oppositions: deaths and divorces or separations.
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Sun-Saturn / Saturn-Sun**: In this direction, we find the death of the individual or of their relatives and friends (especially of males).
- **Sun-Uranus / Uranus-Sun**: Occasionally, in some conjunctions, squares and oppositions, we find some negative events: deaths of family or friends.
- **Sun-Neptune / Neptune-Sun**: We always find deaths, both of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Sun-Pluto / Pluto-Sun**: On a personal level, we find setbacks of all kinds: deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Moon-Mars / Mars-Moon**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: We observe deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: In such cases, we find deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Moon-Neptune / Neptune-Moon**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Moon-Pluto / Pluto-Moon**: We come across deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: On a personal level, events such as family deaths are found.
- **Mercury-Mars / Mars-Mercury**: Deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives or friends.
- **Mercury-Uranus / Uranus-Mercury**: In such cases, we find professional and personal setbacks like accidents or deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Mercury-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mercury**: On a personal level, various problems and deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Venus-Saturn / Saturn-Venus**: Deaths of relatives and friends are common.
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: Sometimes, these directions generate negative events: deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Venus-Neptune / Neptune-Venus**: We also find deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: Among the negative events, we come across deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Mars-Saturn / Saturn-Mars**: We also observe numerous deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: On a personal level, its effect is also very unfavorable: we find many deaths of relatives.
- **Mars-Neptune / Neptune-Mars**: We come across many deaths of friends or family.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: On a personal level, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Mars-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mars**: This direction also often signals the death of the individual or of their friends or relatives.
- **Mars-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mars**: Deaths of friends or family are generally observed.
- **Jupiter-Saturn / Saturn-Jupiter**: Some very negative ones: from unfavorable trips to deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: On a personal level, we find deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Jupiter-Pluto / Pluto-Jupiter**: In some cases, this direction relates to negative events: deaths of friends or family.
- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: Among negative events, there may be the death of a friend or relative.
- **Saturn-Uranus / Uranus-Saturn**: On a personal level, its effect is also very negative; there may be numerous deaths (of the individual or of their family and friends).
- **Saturn-Neptune / Neptune-Saturn**: On a personal level, its effect is negative as well; we find many deaths of friends or family.
- **Saturn-Pluto / Pluto-Saturn**: On a personal level, we find deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Saturn-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Saturn**: We also observe deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Saturn-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Saturn**: On a personal level, we find accidents, deaths of friends or family, and other setbacks.
- **Uranus-Neptune / Neptune-Uranus**: On a personal level, its effect is negative as well; we find numerous deaths of friends or family.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: We also observe some deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: We also find negative events: deaths of friends and relatives.
- **Neptune-Pluto / Pluto-Neptune**: On a personal level, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Neptune-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Neptune**: On a personal level, we find more events, all of them unfavorable: deaths (of the individual or of their relatives or friends).
- **Neptune-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Neptune**: It also usually indicates deaths of friends or family members.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: On a personal level, more events appear, both positive and negative; births and deaths (of the individual or of their relatives and friends) are frequent.
- **Pluto-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Pluto**: We find many more personal events: deaths of friends or family members.
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: Deaths of friends and relatives are more common.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Death of parents: MC for the father. Saturn and the Sun for the father. Naturally, other cusps and planets may be involved, according to the circumstances of the event. The AS with Saturn is frequent (for both the death of the father or the mother), symbolizing mourning and great grief.

# Death of Mother or Grandmother

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H4, H1, H8, MON, VEN, SAT, NEP, PLU, MAR, NNO  
**SECONDARY**: H12, SUN, URA

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find deaths of relatives or friends.
- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find deaths of people surrounding the individual.
- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: We find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Saturn / Saturn-AS**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: In inharmonic aspects and in some conjunctions, we come across some deaths of relatives and friends.
- **AS-Neptune / Neptune-AS**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: In this direction, we find personal and professional setbacks of all types, illnesses, accidents or deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: We also find negative events, such as the ones that occur with the square and some oppositions: illnesses, deaths, divorces and setbacks of all kinds.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: In the squares, in some oppositions and occasionally in some conjunctions, we observe negative events of different types: deaths of relatives or friends (especially of females or of children).
- **MC-Venus / Venus-MC**: Very rarely, the square and even the conjunction and the opposition entail deaths of family or friends, especially of females.
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: In this direction, we find deaths, both of the individual and of their family and friends.
- **MC-Saturn / Saturn-MC**: In this direction, we find personal events such as deaths, both of the individual and of their family and friends.
- **MC-Neptune / Neptune-MC**: On a personal level, it is also common to find problems of all kinds: deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: On a personal level, it often also comes across in problems of all types: deaths either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: We also come across negative events, such as the ones that occur in squares and in some oppositions: deaths and divorces or separations.
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Sun-Uranus / Uranus-Sun**: Occasionally, in some conjunctions, squares and oppositions, we find some negative events: deaths of family or friends.
- **Sun-Neptune / Neptune-Sun**: We always find deaths, both of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Sun-Pluto / Pluto-Sun**: On a personal level, we find setbacks of all kinds: deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Moon-Mars / Mars-Moon**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends. Negative events in the family or with females are possible.
- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: We observe deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: In such cases, we find deaths of relatives or friends, especially of females.
- **Moon-Neptune / Neptune-Moon**: In this direction, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Moon-Pluto / Pluto-Moon**: We come across deaths, either of the individual or of their family and friends.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: On a personal level, events such as family deaths are found.
- **Mercury-Mars / Mars-Mercury**: Deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives or friends.
- **Mercury-Uranus / Uranus-Mercury**: In such cases, we find professional and personal setbacks like accidents or deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Mercury-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mercury**: On a personal level, various problems and deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Venus-Saturn / Saturn-Venus**: Deaths of relatives and friends are common, especially of females.
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: Sometimes, these directions generate negative events: deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Venus-Neptune / Neptune-Venus**: We also find deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: We also find negative events related to this direction: deaths of friends, siblings or children (especially of females).
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: Among the negative events, we come across deaths of relatives or friends.
- **Mars-Saturn / Saturn-Mars**: We also observe numerous deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: On a personal level, its effect is also very unfavorable: we find many deaths of relatives.
- **Mars-Neptune / Neptune-Mars**: We come across many deaths of friends or family.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: On a personal level, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Mars-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mars**: This direction also often signals the death of the individual or of their friends or relatives.
- **Mars-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mars**: Deaths of friends or family are generally observed.
- **Jupiter-Saturn / Saturn-Jupiter**: Some very negative ones: from unfavorable trips to deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: On a personal level, we find deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Jupiter-Pluto / Pluto-Jupiter**: In some cases, this direction relates to negative events: deaths of friends or family.
- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: Among negative events, there may be the death of a friend or relative.
- **Saturn-Uranus / Uranus-Saturn**: On a personal level, its effect is also very negative; there may be numerous deaths (of the individual or of their family and friends).
- **Saturn-Neptune / Neptune-Saturn**: On a personal level, its effect is negative as well; we find many deaths of friends or family.
- **Saturn-Pluto / Pluto-Saturn**: On a personal level, we find deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Saturn-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Saturn**: We also observe deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Saturn-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Saturn**: On a personal level, we find accidents, deaths of friends or family, and other setbacks.
- **Uranus-Neptune / Neptune-Uranus**: On a personal level, its effect is negative as well; we find numerous deaths of friends or family.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: We also observe some deaths of relatives and friends.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: We also find negative events: deaths of friends and relatives.
- **Neptune-Pluto / Pluto-Neptune**: On a personal level, we find deaths, either of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **Neptune-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Neptune**: On a personal level, we find more events, all of them unfavorable: deaths (of the individual or of their relatives or friends).
- **Neptune-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Neptune**: It also usually indicates deaths of friends or family members.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: On a personal level, more events appear, both positive and negative; births and deaths (of the individual or of their relatives and friends) are frequent.
- **Pluto-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Pluto**: We find many more personal events: deaths of friends or family members.
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: Deaths of friends and relatives are more common.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Death of parents: IC for the mother. The Moon and Venus for the mother. Naturally, other cusps and planets may be involved, according to the circumstances of the event. The AS with Saturn is frequent (for both the death of the father or the mother), symbolizing mourning and great grief.

# Failure or Defeat

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H3, SAT, NEP, NNO, MAR, SUN  
**SECONDARY**: H12, URA, MON, MER, PLU

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find setbacks of all kinds.
- **AS-Mercury / Mercury-AS**: Problems, adverse circumstances.
- **AS-Venus / Venus-AS**: Very rarely, the square can involve some personal or professional setbacks.
- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: In this direction, we find personal and professional failures.
- **AS-Jupiter / Jupiter-AS**: Very rarely, the square can involve some negative event, such as a legal setback, but it is not frequent.
- **AS-Saturn / Saturn-AS**: In this direction, we find personal and professional failures. For a politician, it indicates an adverse outcome in an election.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: In inharmonic aspects and in some conjunctions, we come across problems of all types, personal or professional setbacks.
- **AS-Neptune / Neptune-AS**: In this direction, we find personal and professional failures, problems of all kinds. Any unfavorable event is possible, from a negative change of job to an unfortunate trip.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: In this direction, we find personal and professional setbacks of all types.
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: We also find negative events, such as the ones that occur with the square and some oppositions: setbacks of all kinds.
- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: The square can generate professional setbacks or even personal ones.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: In the squares, in some oppositions and occasionally in some conjunctions, we observe professional setbacks.
- **MC-Venus / Venus-MC**: Very rarely, the square and even the conjunction and the opposition entail a professional or personal setback.
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: In this direction, we find failures, professional problems and personal setbacks of all kinds.
- **MC-Saturn / Saturn-MC**: In this direction, we find failures and negative professional changes. For a politician, it indicates an adverse result in an election.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: With the square and to a lesser extent with the conjunction and the opposition, we come across failures and professional setbacks, and also personal setbacks.
- **MC-Neptune / Neptune-MC**: In this direction, we find failures, defeats or professional problems.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: This direction appears in case of failures, professional problems.
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: We also come across negative events, such as the ones that occur in squares and in some oppositions: professional failures, family conflicts.
- **MC-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-MC**: In the opposition, we find some negative events: professional failures, deaths, various setbacks and others.
- **Sun-Moon / Moon-Sun**: Occasionally, the square involves a professional or personal setback.
- **Sun-Mercury / Mercury-Sun**: Sometimes, the square involves a professional or personal setback.
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: In this direction, we find failures, professional problems and personal setbacks of all types.
- **Sun-Saturn / Saturn-Sun**: In this direction, we find professional failures and personal setbacks of all types.
- **Sun-Uranus / Uranus-Sun**: Occasionally, in some conjunctions, squares and oppositions, we find some negative events: professional setbacks.
- **Sun-Neptune / Neptune-Sun**: We always find professional failures and personal setbacks.
- **Sun-Pluto / Pluto-Sun**: We observe failures, tensions or professional problems.
- **Sun-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Sun**: Among the negative events, we can expect all kinds of professional and personal setbacks.
- **Sun-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Sun**: In the opposition we find some negative events: professional failures and different mishaps.
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: Sometimes, inharmonic aspects may lead to a professional or personal misfortune.
- **Moon-Venus / Venus-Moon**: Exceptionally, the inharmonic aspects can involve a professional or personal setback, but it is very rare.
- **Moon-Mars / Mars-Moon**: In this direction, we find failures, professional problems and personal setbacks of all kinds.
- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: We observe failures, personal or professional problems and setbacks of all kinds.
- **Moon-Uranus / Uranus-Moon**: In such cases, we find professional and personal setbacks.
- **Moon-Neptune / Neptune-Moon**: In this direction, we find professional failures and problems, as well as personal setbacks of all kinds.
- **Moon-Pluto / Pluto-Moon**: We come across failures and professional problems.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: Among the negative events, we observe all kinds of professional problems, from failures to resignations.
- **Moon-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Moon**: In the opposition and in some conjunctions, we find some negative events: various mishaps.
- **Mercury-Mars / Mars-Mercury**: In this direction, we find failures, professional problems and personal setbacks of all kinds.
- **Mercury-Saturn / Saturn-Mercury**: In this direction, we find failures, personal or professional problems and setbacks of all kinds.
- **Mercury-Uranus / Uranus-Mercury**: In such cases, we find professional and personal setbacks.
- **Mercury-Neptune / Neptune-Mercury**: In this direction, we find failures, personal or professional problems, and setbacks of all kinds.
- **Mercury-Pluto / Pluto-Mercury**: On a professional level, professional failures of all kinds are found.
- **Mercury-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mercury**: Among the negative events, we come across all kinds of professional problems.
- **Venus-Mars / Mars-Venus**: On a professional level, successes are more frequent than failures.
- **Venus-Saturn / Saturn-Venus**: On a professional level, failures and setbacks are probable. On a personal level, we find some illnesses and disruptions or problems in relationships.
- **Venus-Neptune / Neptune-Venus**: On a professional level, failures and several setbacks are common.
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: Some occasional setbacks are also observed.
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: Among the negative events, we come across all kinds of professional problems and, on a personal level, a variety of problems.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: We also find negative events, both professional and personal, especially with conjunctions and with inharmonic aspects. We also find negative events with inharmonic aspects: legal problems.
- **Mars-Saturn / Saturn-Mars**: This is a very unfavorable direction. On a professional level, we find failures and problems of all kinds. Among other possible setbacks. Family problems.
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: On a professional level, all kinds of failures or interruptions can occur. Specific problems, such as a lawsuit or others.
- **Mars-Neptune / Neptune-Mars**: On a professional level, we find many failures and lots of problems of all kinds, from mistakes to attacks by other people. Among other setbacks.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: On a professional level, we observe failures, defeats, problems, and jobs that come to an end, among others. Problems of all kinds can occur: conflicts with the law.
- **Mars-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mars**: We always find professional failures: from defeats to legal problems.
- **Mars-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mars**: On a professional level, we find many more failures than successes. Divorces and other setbacks are generally observed.
- **Jupiter-Saturn / Saturn-Jupiter**: Nevertheless, failures, defeats and setbacks are also possible.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: Failures and various problems can occur on a professional level.
- **Saturn-Uranus / Uranus-Saturn**: On a professional level, we find failures, problems of all kinds or negative changes.
- **Saturn-Neptune / Neptune-Saturn**: On a professional level, we find failures, hostilities and problems of all kinds.
- **Saturn-Pluto / Pluto-Saturn**: On a professional level, we observe failures, defeats, attacks, and all kinds of problems.
- **Saturn-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Saturn**: On a professional level, failures and problems of all kinds can occur. On a personal level, we find more events, all of them unfavorable. All kinds of adverse personal experiences.
- **Saturn-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Saturn**: We only find negative events. On a professional level, failures, defeats are common. On a personal level, we find other setbacks.
- **Uranus-Neptune / Neptune-Uranus**: On a professional level, failures are possible.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: On a professional level, failures and all kinds of problems are possible.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: On a professional level, we find both successes and defeats: various failures and problems.
- **Neptune-Pluto / Pluto-Neptune**: On a professional level, we observe failures, defeats, and all kinds of problems.
- **Neptune-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Neptune**: On a professional level, we can only expect negative events. On a personal level, we find more events, all of them unfavorable: other adverse experiences.
- **Neptune-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Neptune**: On a professional level, failures and problems of all kinds are observed. On a personal level, we find more events, all unfavorable: other mishaps.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: On a professional level, we find failures.
- **Pluto-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Pluto**: On a professional level, failures and problems are observed. To a lesser extent illnesses and other minor setbacks.
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: On a professional level, failures can take place.

## Other

Death sentence: AS in Jupiter and Saturn combinations. Further afflictions of Jupiter by Saturn (death by jurisdiction). Execution (death): the AS with Pluto or Mars. Combinations with malefics.

Degradation: MC (or AS) with Saturn. MC afflictions of the Sun. Sun-Saturn combinations.

Depression: AS (or XII house cusp) with Saturn. Combinations with the Moon, Saturn and Neptune.

Gossip: XII house cusp, AS or MC, mainly with Mercury or Neptune.

Humiliation: AS or MC in connection with Saturn or in unfavorable aspects to the Sun. Sun-Neptune combinations.

Hunger strike: AS or XII house cusp with Saturn are very typical for events of this kind.

Scandal: Mercury, Mars, Neptune with MC if a public affair. Sex scandal mainly characterized by Neptune with the V house cusp or AS.

Theft (for the loser): II house cusp with Mars or Neptune. Interplanetary combinations between Mercury, Mars and Neptune.

# Arrest

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H12, SAT, URA, NEP, MAR, PLU, NNO  
**SECONDARY**: H3, SUN, MER

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Saturn / Saturn-AS**: Occasionally, it involves arrests or convictions.
- **Sun-Neptune / Neptune-Sun**: All kinds of negative events: from economic sanctions to going to prison.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: We also find negative events with inharmonic aspects: legal problems.
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: Specific problems, such as a lawsuit or others.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: Problems of all kinds can occur: conflicts with the law.
- **Mars-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mars**: We always find professional failures: from defeats to legal problems.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: Legal problems.
- **Jupiter-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Jupiter**: Among negative events, there may be an occasional legal problem.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

**Arrest** (imprisonment): XII house cusp (or AS). With Uranus, sudden. With Saturn, longlasting.

# Losses

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H2, NEP, URA, MER, MAR, SAT  
**SECONDARY**: PLU, NNO

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **Sun-Neptune / Neptune-Sun**: Negative events: from economic sanctions.
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: Negative events: financial problems, for example.
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: Negative events related to this direction: financial problems.
- **Venus-Saturn / Saturn-Venus**: It is a negative direction for monetary issues.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Monetary losses: By speculation: V house cusp with Uranus and additional planets characterizing the event. Also Uranus with AS or MC. By bankruptcy: AS, MC or II house cusp with Neptune and Mercury.

Theft (for the loser): II house cusp with Mars or Neptune. Interplanetary combinations between Mercury, Mars and Neptune.

# Divorce or Separation

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H4, H7, MAR, SAT, NEP, NNO, PLU  
**SECONDARY**: H12, MER, URA, VEN, MON, PLU

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find separations and divorces.
- **AS-Saturn / Saturn-AS**: Occasionally, it involves separations or divorces.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: In the squares, in some oppositions and occasionally in some conjunctions, we observe negative events of different types: divorces or separations.
- **MC-Saturn / Saturn-MC**: At times, it appears in case of separations and divorces, or breakups with partners at a professional level.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: We also observe divorces or separations in all types of aspects.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: As well as in divorces and separations.
- **MC-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-MC**: Negative events, such as the ones that occur in squares and in some oppositions: deaths and divorces or separations.
- **Sun-Saturn / Saturn-Sun**: Even divorces or separations.
- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: Divorces and separations are possible.
- **Moon-Neptune / Neptune-Moon**: With this direction, divorces and marriage separations are common.
- **Mercury-Saturn / Saturn-Mercury**: Separations and divorces.
- **Venus-Uranus / Uranus-Venus**: Sometimes, these directions generate negative events: separations or divorces.
- **Venus-Neptune / Neptune-Venus**: We also find some separations and divorces, but to a lesser extent.
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: Divorces or separations.
- **Venus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Venus**: Among the negative events, we come across divorces or separations.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: We also find negative events with inharmonic aspects: resignations, divorces or separations.
- **Mars-Saturn / Saturn-Mars**: We also observe some divorces or separations.
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: To a lesser extent some divorces.
- **Mars-Neptune / Neptune-Mars**: Occasionally a divorce.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: Divorces, among others.
- **Mars-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Mars**: Divorces are generally observed.
- **Jupiter-Pluto / Pluto-Jupiter**: This direction relates to negative events: divorces, among others.
- **Saturn-Uranus / Uranus-Saturn**: Occasionally, a divorce or separation.
- **Saturn-Neptune / Neptune-Saturn**: At times, this direction signals a divorce and other negative events.
- **Saturn-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Saturn**: Less frequently, separations or divorces.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: Occasionally, some separations or divorces.
- **Neptune-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Neptune**: On a personal level... unfavorable: separations and divorces.
- **Neptune-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Neptune**: It also usually indicates a separation or divorce.
- **Pluto-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Pluto**: Sometimes a separation or divorce.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Divorce: The separation which in most cases precedes a divorce is characterized by the DS (or AS) with Mars or Uranus if sudden. A frequent direction for a divorce is: DS (or AS) in any aspect to Jupiter (law, court)

# Resign or Retire

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H3, H12, SAT, NEP, SUN, MAR, NNO  
**SECONDARY**: URA, MON, MER, PLU

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **MC-Saturn / Saturn-MC**: We find failures, resignations, retirements. For a politician, it indicates a resignation.
- **MC-Neptune / Neptune-MC**: In this direction, we find failures, defeats, resignations or professional problems.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: This direction appears in case of failures, professional problems or resignations.
- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: We observe failures, resignations.
- **Moon-Pluto / Pluto-Moon**: We come across failures, resignations.
- **Mercury-Saturn / Saturn-Mercury**: In this direction, we find failures, resignations.
- **Venus-Neptune / Neptune-Venus**: On a professional level, failures, resignations and several setbacks are common.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: We also find negative events with inharmonic aspects: resignations.
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: On a professional level, all kinds of failures, resignations or interruptions can occur.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: Resignations and various problems can occur on a professional level.
- **Saturn-Neptune / Neptune-Saturn**: On a professional level, we find failures, cessations or resignations.
- **Saturn-Pluto / Pluto-Saturn**: On a professional level, we observe failures, defeats, attacks, resignations.
- **Saturn-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Saturn**: On a professional level, failures, defeats or resignations are common.
- **Uranus-Neptune / Neptune-Uranus**: On a professional level, failures, cessations or resignations are possible.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: On a professional level, failures, resignations and all kinds of problems are possible.
- **Neptune-Pluto / Pluto-Neptune**: On a professional level, we observe failures, defeats, resignations.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Resignation: MC or AS with Saturn (or sometimes the Sun).

# Mobilization

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H12, MAR, SAT, PLU  
**SECONDARY**: H3, MON, MER

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: Events such as military drafts are also found.
- **Moon-Pluto / Pluto-Moon**: Occasionally, events such as military drafts are observed.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: Small negative events such as a military draft.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

**Called up for military service**: MC or AS with Mars and planets according to the circumstances of the event. If under bad (especially psychological) conditions, the participation of the XII cusp and (or) Saturn is frequent.

# Accident

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H3, H12, MAR, URA, SAT, MER  
**SECONDARY**: NEP, NNO, PLU, MON

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: We find personal and professional failures, problems of all kinds, accidents.
- **AS-Saturn / Saturn-AS**: In this direction, we find accidents.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: In inharmonic aspects and in some conjunctions, we come across problems of all types, accidents.
- **AS-Neptune / Neptune-AS**: In this direction, we find accidents.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: In this direction, we find personal and professional setbacks of all types, accidents.
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **MC-Saturn / Saturn-MC**: Personal events such as accidents.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: Personal setbacks: from illnesses and accidents to deaths.
- **MC-Neptune / Neptune-MC**: On a personal level, it is also common to find problems of all kinds: accidents.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: On a personal level, it often also comes across in problems of all types: accidents.
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: Personal setbacks of all types, such as accidents.
- **Sun-Saturn / Saturn-Sun**: Personal setbacks of all types, such as accidents.
- **Sun-Neptune / Neptune-Sun**: We always find personal setbacks, such as accidents.
- **Sun-Pluto / Pluto-Sun**: On a personal level, we find setbacks of all kinds: accidents.
- **Moon-Mars / Mars-Moon**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: Setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **Moon-Neptune / Neptune-Moon**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **Moon-Pluto / Pluto-Moon**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: On a personal level, events such as accidents are found.
- **Mercury-Mars / Mars-Mercury**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **Mercury-Saturn / Saturn-Mercury**: Personal or professional problems and setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **Mercury-Uranus / Uranus-Mercury**: Personal setbacks like accidents.
- **Mercury-Neptune / Neptune-Mercury**: Setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents.
- **Mars-Jupiter / Jupiter-Mars**: Negative events, both professional and personal... with inharmonic aspects: sports accidents. As an example: Jupiter 90° Mars: skiing accident (Juan Carlos I).
- **Mars-Saturn / Saturn-Mars**: On a personal level, its effect is very harmful: we find accidents (sometimes with bone fractures).
- **Mars-Neptune / Neptune-Mars**: On a personal level, its effect is also negative: we come across many illnesses and accidents.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: On a personal level, we find illnesses, accidents, assaults and deaths.
- **Mars-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Mars**: We always find professional failures: from defeats to legal problems, illnesses and accidents.
- **Saturn-Neptune / Neptune-Saturn**: Some accidents can also occur.
- **Saturn-Pluto / Pluto-Saturn**: Among other minor adverse events, there may be illnesses, numerous accidents.
- **Saturn-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Saturn**: On a personal level, we find more events, all of them unfavorable: illnesses and accidents are relatively common.
- **Saturn-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Saturn**: On a personal level, we find accidents.
- **Uranus-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Uranus**: We also find negative events: accidents, among others.
- **Neptune-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Neptune**: On a personal level, we find more events, all unfavorable: illnesses, surgeries and accidents.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

**Accidents**: AS with Mars and Uranus. Falls: Mars-Saturn. Severe painful injuries: Saturn-Uranus.

Wounded: AS with Mars or Uranus. Interplanetary directions with Mars or Uranus. With the consequence of amputation: the AS with Pluto.
