# Hospitalization or Illness

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H12, SAT, NEP, MAR  
**SECONDARY**: PLU, MON

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Sun / Sun-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find some illnesses.
- **AS-Moon / Moon-AS**: In inharmonic aspects, we find illnesses of the individual or of their relatives and friends.
- **AS-Mars / Mars-AS**: In this direction, we find illnesses, surgeries.
- **AS-Saturn / Saturn-AS**: In this direction, we find illnesses.
- **AS-Uranus / Uranus-AS**: In inharmonic aspects and in some conjunctions, we come across illnesses.
- **AS-Neptune / Neptune-AS**: In this direction, we find illnesses.
- **AS-Pluto / Pluto-AS**: In this direction, we find illnesses.
- **AS-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-AS**: We also find negative events, such as illnesses.
- **MC-Sun / Sun-MC**: The square can generate personal ones, such as illnesses.
- **MC-Moon / Moon-MC**: In the squares, in some oppositions and occasionally in some conjunctions, we observe illnesses.
- **MC-Mercury / Mercury-MC**: In the squares, in some oppositions, and at times in some conjunctions, we observe negative events such as illnesses.
- **MC-Mars / Mars-MC**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as illnesses, surgeries.
- **MC-Saturn / Saturn-MC**: Personal events such as illnesses, surgeries.
- **MC-Uranus / Uranus-MC**: Personal setbacks: from illnesses and accidents to deaths.
- **MC-Neptune / Neptune-MC**: On a personal level, it is also common to find problems of all kinds: illnesses, surgeries.
- **MC-Pluto / Pluto-MC**: On a personal level, it often also comes across in problems of all types: illnesses, surgeries.
- **Sun-Moon / Moon-Sun**: Occasionally, the square involves a professional or personal setback, including illnesses.
- **Sun-Mars / Mars-Sun**: Personal setbacks of all types, such as illnesses, surgeries.
- **Sun-Saturn / Saturn-Sun**: Personal setbacks of all types, such as illnesses, surgeries.
- **Sun-Uranus / Uranus-Sun**: Occasionally, in some conjunctions, squares and oppositions, we find some negative events: illnesses or surgeries.
- **Sun-Neptune / Neptune-Sun**: We always find personal setbacks, such as illnesses, surgeries.
- **Sun-Pluto / Pluto-Sun**: On a personal level, we find setbacks of all kinds: illnesses, surgeries.
- **Sun-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Sun**: Among the negative events, we can expect illnesses and other adverse incidents.
- **Moon-Mercury / Mercury-Moon**: Sometimes, inharmonic aspects may lead to a professional or personal misfortune, such as illnesses or others.
- **Moon-Mars / Mars-Moon**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as illnesses, surgeries.
- **Moon-Saturn / Saturn-Moon**: Setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents, illnesses, surgeries.
- **Moon-Neptune / Neptune-Moon**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents, illnesses, surgeries.
- **Moon-Pluto / Pluto-Moon**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as illnesses, surgeries.
- **Moon-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Moon**: In the opposition and in some conjunctions, we find some negative events: illnesses.
- **Mercury-Mars / Mars-Mercury**: Personal setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents, illnesses, surgeries.
- **Mercury-Saturn / Saturn-Mercury**: Personal or professional problems and setbacks of all kinds, such as illnesses.
- **Mercury-Neptune / Neptune-Mercury**: Setbacks of all kinds, such as accidents, illnesses.
- **Venus-Mars / Mars-Venus**: We also find some illnesses and surgeries.
- **Venus-Neptune / Neptune-Venus**: On a personal level, illnesses and accidents are relatively common.
- **Venus-Pluto / Pluto-Venus**: We also find negative events related to this direction: some diseases.
- **Venus-Saturn / Saturn-Venus**: On a personal level, we find some illnesses.
- **Mars-Saturn / Saturn-Mars**: On a personal level, its effect is very harmful: we find illnesses.
- **Mars-Uranus / Uranus-Mars**: On a personal level, its effect is also very unfavorable: we find quite a lot of diseases.
- **Mars-Neptune / Neptune-Mars**: On a personal level, its effect is also negative: we come across many illnesses... and occasionally a health issue in the family.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: On a personal level, we find illnesses.
- **Jupiter-Neptune / Neptune-Jupiter**: On a personal level, we find illnesses, surgeries.
- **Saturn-Uranus / Uranus-Saturn**: On a personal level, its effect is also very negative; there may be diseases, surgeries.
- **Saturn-Neptune / Neptune-Saturn**: Illnesses (of the individual or of their relatives) can also occur.
- **Saturn-Pluto / Pluto-Saturn**: Among other minor adverse events, there may be illnesses.
- **Saturn-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Saturn**: Unfavorable: illnesses and accidents are relatively common.
- **Uranus-Neptune / Neptune-Uranus**: On a personal level, its effect is negative as well; Illnesses and surgeries are also observed.
- **Uranus-Pluto / Pluto-Uranus**: On a personal level, we find illnesses and surgeries.
- **Neptune-Pluto / Pluto-Neptune**: On a personal level, we find many illnesses, some surgeries.
- **Neptune-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Neptune**: On a personal level, we find more events, all unfavorable: illnesses, surgeries.
- **Pluto-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Pluto**: To a lesser extent illnesses and other minor setbacks.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

**Amputations** (surgical): AS, Mars, Pluto and Saturn.

Hospitalization: XII house cusp or AS, mainly with Saturn. Other planets (such as Uranus: sudden) characterize the circumstances of the event.

Insanity: AS or XII house cusp with the Moon, Mercury, Saturn and Neptune.

Operation (surgical): AS with Mars. Naturally other planets may also be involved according to the circumstances of the event. If the wounds won't heal (sepsis), Mars-Neptune combinations. With Saturn, general delay or long stay in hospital.

# Violence

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H12, MAR, PLU, SAT, URA  
**SECONDARY**: MER

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **Mars-Saturn / Saturn-Mars**: Any kind of physical aggression.
- **Mars-Pluto / Pluto-Mars**: On a personal level, we find illnesses, accidents, assaults and deaths.
- **Saturn-Pluto / Pluto-Saturn**: At times some kind of aggression.
- **Lunar Node-Part of Fortune / Part of Fortune-Lunar Node**: Can even indicate the death of the person and some sort of violence.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Degradation: MC (or AS) with Saturn. MC afflictions of the Sun. Sun-Saturn combinations.

Violence: AS, Mars, Saturn and Uranus in various combinations. Rape or sex murder: Venus, Mars, Pluto and Saturn. The symbolism appears to be the same for the victim as well as for the murderer. The XII house cusp with Saturn may be involved in the birth chart of the murderer (XII house: hiding, Saturn: fear).

Wounded: AS with Mars or Uranus. Interplanetary directions with Mars or Uranus. With the consequence of amputation: the AS with Pluto.

# Intrigue

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H12, NEP, MER  
**SECONDARY**: PLU, MAR

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **Saturn-Uranus / Uranus-Saturn**: On a professional level, we find negative changes, including scandals and intrigues.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Gossip: XII house cusp, AS or MC, mainly with Mercury or Neptune.

Intrigue: AS or XII house cusp, mainly with Neptune or Mercury.

Scandal: Mercury, Mars, Neptune with MC if a public affair. Sex scandal mainly characterized by Neptune with the V house cusp or AS.

# Gambling Loss

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H2, H5, NEP, SAT, URA, MAR  
**SECONDARY**: PLU, NNO

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

None

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Monetary losses: By speculation: V house cusp with Uranus and additional planets characterizing the event. Also Uranus with AS or MC. By bankruptcy: AS, MC or II house cusp with Neptune and Mercury.

# Negative Travel

## KEY PLANETARY INDICATORS (STARKMAN)

**PRIMARY**: H10, H1, H9, SAT, MAR, NEP, PLU, MER, URA  
**SECONDARY**: H12

## Astrological Combinations (Estadella)

- **AS-Neptune / Neptune-AS**: An unfortunate trip.
- **Moon-Lunar Node / Lunar Node-Moon**: On a personal level, events such as unfavorable trips are found.
- **Jupiter-Saturn / Saturn-Jupiter**: Some very negative ones: from unfavorable trips to deaths of relatives and friends.

## Summary of Astrological Signatures (Marr)

Voyage (trips, journeys): III or IX house cusp with the Moon, Mercury or Uranus. Neptune overseas (also flights). Jupiter stands for long journeys (or stays) in foreign countries. Uranus symbolizes new impressions, incidents and accidents.
